1,395 entries, compose to configure. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **compose-add-example**: Add one new example sentence to a sense, with its text in one or more writing systems and optional translations. To bring an example over from another sense, use `compose-move-example`. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/compose-add-example.md) · ★1)
- **compose-add-exception-features**: Use this command to tag many stems with the same exception features, or to add the features an inflectional or derivational affix requires, in one call. It adds to what each entry already has and removes nothing; `compose-edit-exception-features` replaces a whole set for one entry instead. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/compose-add-exception-features.md) · ★1)
- **compose-add-morphosyntax-analysis**: Use this command when one existing form should work in two grammatical roles, such as a Verb suffix that must also attach to Copula stems. FieldWorks holds this as one entry with two grammatical infos (morphosyntactic analyses), and each sense points at one of them. The parser loads every analysis of an entry as its own rule, so the same form parses on both categories, and the glosses of the two s ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/compose-add-morphosyntax-analysis.md) · ★1)
- **compose-add-phonological-feature-value**: Stages one more value for a closed phonological feature that exists, such as `velar` for `place`. Use the closed input `{ "feature": "&lt;canonical ID&gt;", "name": "velar", "abbreviation": "vel" }`; `abbreviation` is optional. The feature must belong to the phonological feature system, not the morphosyntactic one. A value name or abbreviation the feature already has is refused. Only a person can Apply. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/compose-add-phonological-feature-value.md) · ★1)
- **compose-add-source**: Stage a new Source in the project's Motif Sources list. The first one creates the list. The intent gives the id, name, versification, Context and kind; nothing is written until a person applies the pending changes. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/compose-add-source.md) · ★1)
- **compose-agent**: Erstelle einen dynamischen Agent aus dem Trait-System (480 Kombinationen) ([PrimeLineDirekt/Evolving-Template](https://github.com/PrimeLineDirekt/Evolving-Template/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/compose-agent.md))
- **compose-author-adhoc-prohibition**: Create a morpheme or allomorph co-occurrence prohibition, which FieldWorks calls an ad hoc rule. A morpheme prohibition names a first morpheme and the morphemes it cannot occur with; an allomorph prohibition does the same for forms. The parser then drops any analysis that contains the prohibited combination. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/compose-author-adhoc-prohibition.md) · ★1)
- **compose-author-affix-process**: Use this command to stage one new affix whose form is described by a pattern and a realization. An input part names one phoneme, one natural class, or the remaining stem; output items copy named input parts, insert saved or previously staged phonemes, insert one segment of a natural class, or copy an input part with the features of a feature class laid over it. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/compose-author-affix-process.md) · ★1)
- **compose-author-affix-process-allomorph**: Use this command to give an affix entry another pattern-based form, whether its lexeme form is a plain affix allomorph or an affix process, the way Lexicon Edit &gt; Insert Allomorph &gt; Affix Process does in FieldWorks. One call stages the new allomorph on an entry that exists, or that an earlier command added to the same Draft. Its `input` and `output` mean what they mean for `compose-author-affix-pr ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/compose-author-affix-process-allomorph.md) · ★1)
- **compose-author-affix-slot**: Use this command when the existing inflectional slots do not express the intended grammar. It creates a named slot on an existing category and can assign existing inflectional affix MSAs to it in the same Draft. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/compose-author-affix-slot.md) · ★1)
- **compose-author-affix-template**: Use this command when two branches of a category need different required slots. It adds an active alternative template without changing the category's existing templates or their order. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/compose-author-affix-template.md) · ★1)
- **compose-author-allomorph-required-features**: Stages an empty Required Features structure on one ordinary affix allomorph in a Draft. FieldWorks shows this as **Required Features**, on the lexeme form or an alternate form. The closed input is `{ "allomorph": "&lt;form ID&gt;" }`; the form may already exist or have been staged earlier in the Draft. Only a person can Apply. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/compose-author-allomorph-required-features.md) · ★1)
- **compose-author-alternate-form**: Use this command to give an entry another form, such as a prefix that changes before a vowel. One call stages the form on an entry that already exists, or that an earlier command added to the same Draft, and links the conditions you name. It adds no entry, sense or grammatical info and leaves the lexeme form and every other form as they are. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/compose-author-alternate-form.md) · ★1)
- **compose-author-circumfix**: Use this command to stage one new circumfix: a single entry with a prefix part and a suffix part that attach to a stem together, as in Indonesian `ke-...-an`. FieldWorks holds it as one entry of the circumfix type whose lexeme form reads `ke- -an`, with a prefix form and a suffix form beside it. The parser pairs the two parts into one rule, so neither part alone attaches. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/compose-author-circumfix.md) · ★1)
- **compose-author-environment**: Stages an environment with explicitly declared left and right contexts in a Draft. Contexts use project phonemes and natural classes, plus the word boundary `#` at the outer edge. An environment cannot hold a morpheme boundary or any other boundary marker, because the parser ignores such an environment and the form would be unconstrained. To condition a change on a morpheme boundary, use a phonolo ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/compose-author-environment.md) · ★1)
- **compose-author-exception-feature**: Use this command to add an exception feature to the project's Exception Features list. An exception feature is a tag with no meaning of its own: you tag the stems or clitics that belong to a lexical class, then tag the affixes that may attach only to that class. Black's FieldWorks parsing guide (section 2.1.6) uses it to stop an affix that is correct for some stems from also parsing every other st ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/compose-author-exception-feature.md) · ★1)
- **compose-author-feature-structure**: Stages an empty feature structure on a stem, inflectional affix or derivational affix MSA in a Draft. The MSA may already exist in the project or have been authored earlier in the same Draft. Only a person can Apply. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/compose-author-feature-structure.md) · ★1)
- **compose-author-feature-value**: Stages a closed feature specification in an existing or earlier-staged feature structure, including a stem's MsFeatures or an inflectional affix's InflFeats. Inflectional affix selections are word requirements. Only a person can Apply. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/compose-author-feature-value.md) · ★1)
- **compose-author-inflection-class**: Add a named inflection class, such as a noun class or conjugation, to a category. A class can be nested under an existing class in that same category. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/compose-author-inflection-class.md) · ★1)
- **compose-author-inflection-feature**: Add a closed morphosyntactic feature and the values it can take, such as Number with Singular and Plural. One call stages the feature and all its named values in the project's morphosyntactic feature system. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/compose-author-inflection-feature.md) · ★1)
- **compose-author-lexeme-form**: Stages a lexeme form with explicitly authored spelling, writing system and morph type in a Draft. Dry Run and Trial the Draft before Finalize; only a person can Apply. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/compose-author-lexeme-form.md) · ★1)
- **compose-author-lexical-entry**: Use this command to add a stem or an affix the project does not have yet. One call stages a complete entry: its lexeme form, one sense with a gloss and an optional definition, and the grammatical info the parser needs to use it. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/compose-author-lexical-entry.md) · ★1)
- **compose-author-metathesis-rule**: Use this command to stage one metathesis rule in a Draft: two neighbouring sounds that trade places, such as Cebuano *dahm-a* becoming *damh-a* (Wolff 5.14; Black §6.1.2.3). It writes the rule the way FieldWorks does, as a Metathesis Rule whose Rule Formula has a **Left environment**, the two **Switch these items** cells, an optional middle and a **Right environment**. FieldWorks shows the result ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/compose-author-metathesis-rule.md) · ★1)
- **compose-author-natural-class**: Stages a named segment natural class with explicitly declared phoneme members in a Draft. Members may name existing phonemes or phonemes created earlier through declared dependencies. Only a person can Apply. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/compose-author-natural-class.md) · ★1)
- **compose-author-part-of-speech**: Use this command when the project has no category for a kind of word you are about to enter, such as a noun or a transitive verb. It creates the category in the project's parts-of-speech list, or under an existing category as a subcategory, and gives it a name and an optional abbreviation. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/compose-author-part-of-speech.md) · ★1)
- **compose-author-phoneme**: Stages a phoneme with explicitly authored name and representations in a Draft. Use its created identity in later natural classes or phonological rules. Only a person can Apply. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/compose-author-phoneme.md) · ★1)
- **compose-author-phonological-feature**: Stages a closed phonological feature and its values in the project's phonological feature system, such as `place` with `labial`, `coronal` and `velar`. Phonemes then carry the values (see [Set a phoneme's features](cmd:compose-edit-phoneme-features)) and a natural class can be defined by them ([Author a natural class](cmd:compose-author-natural-class), [Edit a natural class's features](cmd:compose ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/compose-author-phonological-feature.md) · ★1)
- **compose-author-phonological-rule**: Use this command to stage one regular phonological rule in a Draft. FieldWorks' Input and Output are the `input` and `output` arrays. Motif accepts up to eight items in each, in order. Each item names an existing `phoneme` or `naturalClass`, or an Output feature `change`. An empty `input` means insertion, and an empty `output` means deletion. A natural class in the input matches any member of that ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/compose-author-phonological-rule.md) · ★1)
- **compose-author-stem-name**: Add a named set of feature conditions describing where a stem allomorph is eligible. A stem name belongs to a category and contains one or more regions; each region is a nonempty set of closed feature/value choices. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/compose-author-stem-name.md) · ★1)
- **compose-author-text**: The closed intent gives the Text's title, vernacular writing system `ws`, and paragraphs. Each paragraph lists baseline pieces in `segments`, joined by single spaces. A piece is an object with `text` and optional `freeTranslation`, `literalTranslation` and `notes` (an ordered array of strings). All text uses NFD. An unknown or repeated property is refused. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/compose-author-text.md) · ★1)
- **compose-author-variant-entry**: Create a sense-less variant of one sense in one step: the entry, its lexeme form with morph type and environments, and a variant reference to the main sense. The parser reads the variant as that sense's stem, with the sense's gloss and analysis, so a second shape of a stem needs no copied sense. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/compose-author-variant-entry.md) · ★1)
- **compose-batch**: Stages every intent in a JSON file in one Draft with one project load, through the same checks as each composing command alone. If any intent is refused, nothing is added. Only a person can Apply. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/compose-batch.md) · ★1)
- **compose-board-pack**: Compose a board pack as a story, not a data dump — open with the headline, cash before profit, variance commentary in line with its table, 5-7 KPI cards on the front pages, explicit decisions/asks on the last body slide, detail in the appendix. ([mcorbett51090/RavenClaude](https://github.com/mcorbett51090/RavenClaude/blob/HEAD/plugins/finance/commands/compose-board-pack.md) · ★9)
- **compose-brief**: Emit a project memory brief from ~/.claude/project-sessions/&lt;slug&gt;/history.jsonl with continuation framing. No preamble. ([mutale/session-normalizer](https://github.com/mutale/session-normalizer/blob/HEAD/commands/compose-brief.md))
- **compose-change-affix-kind**: Use this command when an affix was entered as one kind and the evidence says another: inflectional (it fills a slot of a template), derivational (it makes a word of a category from a word of another), or unclassified (not yet analysed). Choosing and changing later is the normal path, so the change is made in place and can be changed back with the same command. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/compose-change-affix-kind.md) · ★1)
- **compose-citation-check**: Audit citation consistency and accuracy across a manuscript via the compose skill in citation-check mode. ([drader/researcher_agent](https://github.com/drader/researcher_agent/blob/HEAD/commands/compose-citation-check.md) · ★2)
- **compose-code**: Compose a screen in code from the design system's own components and tokens — plan checked before writing, every value a token, gated before it's handed over. ([jrpease/throughline](https://github.com/jrpease/throughline/blob/HEAD/commands/compose-code.md) · ★80)
- **compose-code**: Compose the screen the user asked for as one React file in their app, from what the design system already has. Use the settings already in `design-system.json` rather than re-asking them. Scale explanation to `user.codingLevel`. - **compose-delete-adhoc-prohibition**: `compose-delete-adhoc-prohibition` stages deletion of one flat allomorph or morpheme ad hoc prohibition. MCP offers `motif_delete_adhoc_prohibition`; only a person can Apply. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/compose-delete-adhoc-prohibition.md) · ★1)
- **compose-delete-affix-process**: `compose-delete-affix-process` stages deletion of one affix process with every input, output and other object it owns. Supply `entry` and `form`, with optional `replacement`: an existing plain affix allomorph of the same entry and morph type. A plain replacement can be authored earlier in this Draft with [Add an alternate form](cmd:compose-author-alternate-form). MCP offers `motif_delete_affix_pro ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/compose-delete-affix-process.md) · ★1)
- **compose-delete-empty-sense**: Delete one wholly empty sense of an entry, together with the stem analysis that only that sense uses. A variant entry that keeps one empty sense blocks its main entry's path; this removes the sense so the variant takes it. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/compose-delete-empty-sense.md) · ★1)
- **compose-delete-environment**: Deletes a phonological environment that no allomorph, rule or other object uses. It refuses an environment still in use and names each user. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/compose-delete-environment.md) · ★1)
- **compose-delete-exception-feature**: Deletes an item of the Exception Features list that nothing refers to any more. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/compose-delete-exception-feature.md) · ★1)
- **compose-delete-lexical-entry**: Delete one whole dictionary entry together with its lexeme form, alternate forms, senses, examples, grammatical info and entry references. Use it when the entry is redundant, for example a duplicate whose forms now live on another entry, or an old entry a new one replaces. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/compose-delete-lexical-entry.md) · ★1)
- **compose-delete-natural-class**: Deletes a natural class that nothing uses: no rule or other class refers to it, and no environment string writes its name or abbreviation in square brackets. - **compose-remove-exception-feature**: Use this command to take one exception feature out of the grammar's uses in one step: every stem that carries it, and every phonological rule branch that requires or excludes it. - **compose-remove-feature-value**: Use this command to take one feature's specification off a feature structure: a stem's MsFeatures, so the stem no longer carries the feature, or an inflectional affix's InflFeats, so the affix no longer requires it. It undoes [authoring a feature value](cmd:compose-author-feature-value). ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/compose-remove-feature-value.md) · ★1)
- **compose-retire-affix-slot**: Delete an inflectional affix slot that nothing uses any more. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/compose-retire-affix-slot.md) · ★1)
- **compose-retire-boundary-marker**: Remove a stray second copy of the word boundary (`#`) or morpheme boundary (`+`) marker from the first phoneme set. Motif refuses to author a rule with a boundary while the set holds two markers for the same boundary, because it cannot tell which one the rule should name; retiring the unused copy clears that refusal. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/compose-retire-boundary-marker.md) · ★1)

- **compose-rewrite-lexeme-form**: Rewrites the lexeme forms of existing entries, many in one Draft, preserving each form's identity, references, conditions and other writing-system alternatives. - **compose-set-character-class**: Moves the named characters of one project writing system between word-forming (part of a word) and punctuation (not part of a word). `class` is `wordForming` or `punctuation`; `characters` lists the characters together, one visible character each. Motif refuses punctuation for a letter, mark or modifier, because no writing-system setting can make one so. - **compose-set-no-default-compounding**: The parser joins any two stems into a word by default, even when the project has no compound rules, unless the project's parser settings say otherwise. - **compose-set-not-on-clitics**: The parser runs the project's phonological rules on a word before any clitic attaches to it, unless the project's parser settings say otherwise. - **conclave-adr**: TL ADR authoring outside inception. - **conclave-bug**: Report a bug (post-merge regression) or list the open bug backlog. `report` runs a Haiku pre-analysis on the raw input, asks targeted questions only when ambiguity would change the fix direction, and produces a BUG-NNN artifact with Gherkin repro steps and a suspected code area. When `lab_test.enabled` in config, the Tech Lead also generates an executable e2e lab spec (BUG-NNN-lab.md). Mirrors the bug as a GitHub issue. Hands off directly to /conclave-dev — bugs skip Sprint Planning. `list` is mechanical, no subagent call. - **conclave-close**: Close the active sprint. Writes review.md, retro.md, the closing report, UAT guide and DORA snapshot, and sets the sprint closed. Refuses while a critical bug is open. - **conclave-discovery**: Turn a raw product idea (or an incomplete product document) into the product documentation package Conclave inception needs — discovery (problem, ICP, personas, competitors, UVP, features), tech stack, data model, BLOC (domain rules) and MVP (Product Goal, metrics, candidate epics, Sprint 0, sequencing) — written as plain markdown under docs/product/. - **conclave-epic**: PM epic authoring outside inception. First arg is a sub-action — new (author an epic and slot it into the roadmap), edit EP-NNN (revise), split EP-NNN (decompose into 2–4 epics), retire EP-NNN (drop it). Delegates to the Product Manager subagent for new/edit/split, the Tech Lead for the risk pass (uncertainty, needs_spec, spike candidates), and the Scrum Master for the roadmap update; retire is mechanical. - **conclave-init**: One-time project setup plus Scrum inception. Configures the Conclave workspace (name, story prefix, stack, profile, cadence), then finds your product documentation (or offers /conclave-discovery to write it) and turns it into a product vision with a Product Goal, a set of epics, an architectural foundation, and a sprint-by-sprint roadmap (Sprint 0 walking skeleton first). Run once per repo before /conclave-planning. `--upgrade` migrates a v1.x workspace to the v2 structure. - **conclave-planning**: Sprint Planning for the next roadmap slot. Checks the slot's epics are ready to refine (approved SPEC when needs_spec, spike done or scheduled when uncertainty is high), refines them into INVEST stories with Gherkin acceptance criteria just in time (from the SPEC's story breakdown when there is one), turns the slot's spike entries into timeboxed spike stories, adds carry-over and retro action items, sizes the commitment against real velocity, assigns by discipline, and locks the sprint active. - **conclave-pr-review**: Tech Lead PR review and approval gate for a story or bug (US-NNN or BUG-NNN). - **conclave-qa**: Verify one or more stories and/or bugs (US-NNN or BUG-NNN) against their Gherkin scenarios on the develop branch. QA does NOT approve the PR — that is the Tech Lead's call via /conclave-pr-review. - **conclave-roadmap**: Release planning — how many sprints, and what goes in each. show prints the release plan (sprints planned, Sprint 0, MVP slot, launch risk, spikes, epics beyond the horizon). replan [--sprints N | --sprints auto] has the Scrum Master re-sequence every future planned slot — epics, spike entries ahead of risky epics, dates from current velocity — against a fixed number of sprints or as many as the must/should epics need. - **conclave-spec**: Technical specification for an epic. /conclave-spec EP-NNN has the Tech Lead compose the epic's design — decisions (ADRs), components, contracts, data changes, domain rules, test strategy, rollout and a story breakdown — and the Product Manager check it against the epic's scope; the result is SPEC-NNN in draft. /conclave-spec approve SPEC-NNN marks it approved so /conclave-planning can refine the epic's stories from it. - **cone**: How AutoCAD's CONE draws a 3D solid cone or a frustum, prompt by prompt: base, radius, height, Top radius, Elliptical and Axis endpoint. Try it free online. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/cone.md))
- **cone.ar**: كيف يرسم أمر ⁨CONE⁩ في أوتوكاد مخروطًا مجسمًا أو جذع مخروط: القاعدة ونصف القطر والارتفاع، و⁨Top radius⁩ و⁨Elliptical⁩ و⁨Axis endpoint⁩. جرّبه مجانًا. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/cone.ar.md))
- **cone.cs**: Jak nakreslit 3D kužel nebo komolý kužel v AutoCADu příkazem KUŽEL (CONE): základna, poloměr, výška, Horní poloměr, Eliptický a koncový bod osy. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/cone.cs.md))
- **cone.de**: Wie KEGEL (CONE) in AutoCAD einen 3D-Kegel oder Kegelstumpf zeichnet, Schritt für Schritt: Basis, Radius, Höhe, Oberer Radius, Elliptisch und Achsenendpunkt. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/cone.de.md))
- **cone.es**: Cómo funciona CONO (CONE) de AutoCAD en español, solicitud a solicitud: cono o tronco de cono, base, radio, altura, Radio superior, Elíptico y Eje. Pruébalo. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/cone.es.md))
- **cone.fr**: Comment CONE d'AutoCAD en français dessine un cône ou un tronc de cône : base, rayon, hauteur, rayon supérieur, Elliptique. Essayez-le gratuitement en ligne. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/cone.fr.md))
- **cone.hu**: 3D kúp vagy csonkakúp rajzolása az AutoCAD-ben a KÚP (CONE) paranccsal, promptról promptra: alap, sugár, magasság, Felső sugár, Elliptikus és tengely végpontja. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/cone.hu.md))
- **cone.it**: Come AutoCAD disegna un cono solido 3D o un tronco di cono con CONO (CONE): base, raggio, altezza, Raggio superiore, Ellittico. Provalo. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/cone.it.md))
- **cone.ja**: AutoCAD で 3D ソリッドの円錐や円錐台を作成する CONE の使い方をプロンプト順に解説します。底面、半径、高さ、上面の半径、楕円、軸の端点。ブラウザで無料で試せます。 ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/cone.ja.md))
- **cone.ko**: AutoCAD 원추(CONE)로 3D 솔리드 원추나 절두 원추를 그리는 방법을 프롬프트 순서대로 설명합니다: 밑면, 반지름, 높이, 상단 반지름, 타원형, 축 끝점. 브라우저에서 무료로 해 볼 수 있습니다. ([ovelilla/mellowcad](https://github.com/ovelilla/mellowcad/blob/HEAD/content/commands/cone.ko.md))
